<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

I have $\pounds 45$ in $5 \ \text{p}$ coins. How many $5 \ \text{p}$ coins is that?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">I have $\pounds 45$ in $5 \ \text{p}$ coins. How many $5 \ \text{p}$ coins is that?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Number of coins}     &= \dfrac {\pounds 45} {5 \ \text {p}} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {45 \times 100 \ \text {p}} {5 \ \text {p}} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {4500 \ \text {p}} {5 \ \text {p}} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {900 \times 5} {5} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {900 \times \cancel {5}} {\cancel {5}} \\\\
                            &= 900
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$900$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

I have a bag full of $5 \ \text{p}$ coins, a bag full of $2 \ \text{p}$ coins and a bag full of $1 \ \text{p}$ coins. I take $5$ coins from these bags and get a total of $11 \ \text{p}$.
What were the $5$ coins that I took?



</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">I have a bag full of $5 \ \text{p}$ coins, a bag full of $2 \ \text{p}$ coins and a bag full of $1 \ \text{p}$ coins. I take $5$ coins from these bags and get a total of $11 \ \text{p}$.
What were the $5$ coins that I took?
<emptyline>

</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

|  $11 \ \text{p}$ | Coins                                   |
|:-----------------------:|:----------------------------------------:|
| $5$ coins                 | $5 \ \text{p} + 2 \ \text{p} + 2 \ \text{p} + 1 \ \text{p} + 1 \ \text{p}$ |
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$5 \ \text{p} + 2 \ \text{p} + 2 \ \text{p} + 1 \ \text{p} + 1 \ \text{p}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Find the smallest amount of money that cannot be paid using three or fewer British coins.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Find the smallest amount of money that cannot be paid using three or fewer British coins.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- The coins available are: $1 \ \text{p}$, $2 \ \text{p}$, $5 \ \text{p}$, $10 \ \text{p}$, $20 \ \text{p}$, $50 \ \text{p}$, $\pounds 1$, $\pounds 2$.
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let's find the smallest amount that requires more than $3$ coins. 

We can list the amounts starting from $1 \ \text{p}$ to check the number of coins required.

| Total value     | Coins used                                     | Number of coins |
| :-------------: | :--------------------------------------------: | :-------------: |
| $1 \ \text{p}$  | $1 \ \text{p}$                                 | $1$             |
| $2 \ \text{p}$  | $2 \ \text{p}$                                 | $1$             |
| $3 \ \text{p}$  | $2 \ \text{p} + 1 \ \text{p}$                  | $2$             |
| $4 \ \text{p}$  | $2 \ \text{p} + 2 \ \text{p}$                  | $2$             |
| $5 \ \text{p}$  | $5 \ \text{p}$                                 | $1$             |
| $6 \ \text{p}$  | $5 \ \text{p} + 1 \ \text{p}$                  | $2$             |
| $7 \ \text{p}$  | $5 \ \text{p} + 2 \ \text{p}$                  | $2$             |
| $8 \ \text{p}$  | $5 \ \text{p} + 2 \ \text{p} + 1 \ \text{p}$   | $3$             |
| $9 \ \text{p}$  | $5 \ \text{p} + 2 \ \text{p} + 2 \ \text{p}$   | $3$             |
| $10 \ \text{p}$ | $10 \ \text{p}$                                | $1$             |
| $11 \ \text{p}$ | $10 \ \text{p} + 1 \ \text{p}$                 | $2$             |
| $12 \ \text{p}$ | $10 \ \text{p} + 2 \ \text{p}$                 | $2$             |
| $13 \ \text{p}$ | $10 \ \text{p} + 2 \ \text{p} + 1 \ \text{p}$  | $3$             |
| $14 \ \text{p}$ | $10 \ \text{p} + 2 \ \text{p} + 2 \ \text{p}$  | $3$             |
| $15 \ \text{p}$ | $10 \ \text{p} + 5 \ \text{p}$                 | $2$             |
| $16 \ \text{p}$ | $10 \ \text{p} + 5 \ \text{p} + 1 \ \text{p}$  | $3$             |
| $17 \ \text{p}$ | $10 \ \text{p} + 5 \ \text{p} + 2 \ \text{p}$  | $3$             |
| $18 \ \text{p}$ | Not possible with $3$ coins                    | $>3$            |

Smallest amount $= 18 \ \text{p}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$18 \ \text{p}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_note_md_coins_available
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>

<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Let's find the smallest amount that requires more than $3$ coins. 
<emptyline>
We can list the amounts starting from $1 \ \text{p}$ to check the number of coins required.
<emptyline>
| Total value     | Coins used                                     | Number of coins |
| :-------------: | :--------------------------------------------: | :-------------: |
| $1 \ \text{p}$  | $1 \ \text{p}$                                 | $1$             |
| $2 \ \text{p}$  | $2 \ \text{p}$                                 | $1$             |
| $3 \ \text{p}$  | $2 \ \text{p} + 1 \ \text{p}$                  | $2$             |
| $4 \ \text{p}$  | $2 \ \text{p} + 2 \ \text{p}$                  | $2$             |
| $5 \ \text{p}$  | $5 \ \text{p}$                                 | $1$             |
| $6 \ \text{p}$  | $5 \ \text{p} + 1 \ \text{p}$                  | $2$             |
| $7 \ \text{p}$  | $5 \ \text{p} + 2 \ \text{p}$                  | $2$             |
| $8 \ \text{p}$  | $5 \ \text{p} + 2 \ \text{p} + 1 \ \text{p}$   | $3$             |
| $9 \ \text{p}$  | $5 \ \text{p} + 2 \ \text{p} + 2 \ \text{p}$   | $3$             |
| $10 \ \text{p}$ | $10 \ \text{p}$                                | $1$             |
| $11 \ \text{p}$ | $10 \ \text{p} + 1 \ \text{p}$                 | $2$             |
| $12 \ \text{p}$ | $10 \ \text{p} + 2 \ \text{p}$                 | $2$             |
| $13 \ \text{p}$ | $10 \ \text{p} + 2 \ \text{p} + 1 \ \text{p}$  | $3$             |
| $14 \ \text{p}$ | $10 \ \text{p} + 2 \ \text{p} + 2 \ \text{p}$  | $3$             |
| $15 \ \text{p}$ | $10 \ \text{p} + 5 \ \text{p}$                 | $2$             |
| $16 \ \text{p}$ | $10 \ \text{p} + 5 \ \text{p} + 1 \ \text{p}$  | $3$             |
| $17 \ \text{p}$ | $10 \ \text{p} + 5 \ \text{p} + 2 \ \text{p}$  | $3$             |
| $18 \ \text{p}$ | Not possible with $3$ coins                    | $>3$            |
<emptyline>
Smallest amount $= 18 \ \text{p}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The cost in pounds, $C$, of going to the cinema can be worked out using the formula

$C = 8a + 3k$

In this formula $a$ stands for the number of adults and $k$ stands for the number of children.

</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What is the cost if three adults and one child go to the cinema?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What is the cost if three adults and one child go to the cinema?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total Cost}  &= 8a + 3k \\\\
                    &= 8 \times 3 + 3 \times 1 \\\\
                    &= 24 + 3 \\\\
                    &= \pounds 27
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The total cost for the Bryant family is $\pounds 25$. How many adults and how many children are in the family?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The total cost for the Bryant family is $\pounds 25$. How many adults and how many children are in the family?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
25          &= 8a + 3k \\\\
3k          &= 25 - 8a \\\\
k           &= \dfrac {25 - 8a} {3}
\end{aligned}
$

We can easily infer the answer by using trial and error.

| $a$ (adults) |        $k = \dfrac{25 - 8a}{3}$                | $k$ whole number? |
|:------------:|:---------------------------------------------:|:------------------:|
| $1$          | $\dfrac {25 - 8 \times 1} {3} = \dfrac {17} {3} = 5.67$ | No                |
| $2$          | $\dfrac {25 - 8 \times 2} {3} = \dfrac {9} {3} = 3$     | Yes              |
| $3$          | $\dfrac {25 - 8 \times 3} {3} = \dfrac {1} {3} = 0.33$  | No                |

Adults $= 2$

Using $a = 2$ in the equation for $k$,

$
\begin{aligned}
k           &= \dfrac {25 - 8a} {3} \\\\
k           &= \dfrac {25 - 8 \times 2} {3} \\\\
k           &= \dfrac {25 - 16} {3} \\\\
k           &= \dfrac {9} {3} \\\\
k           &= 3
\end{aligned}
$

Children $= 3$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

adults $=$ $2$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

children $=$ $3$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A calculator is used to divide $\pounds 17.20$ by $9$. The display shows $1.91111111$. How much is $\pounds 17.20 \div 9$, 
to the nearest penny?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A calculator is used to divide $\pounds 17.20$ by $9$. The display shows $1.91111111$. How much is $\pounds 17.20 \div 9$, 
to the nearest penny?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Display} &= 1.91111111 \\
\text{Nearest penny} &= \pounds 1.91
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 1.91$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A drink and a box of popcorn together cost $90 \ \text{p}$. Two drinks and a box of popcorn together cost $\pounds 1.45$. What does a box of popcorn cost?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A drink and a box of popcorn together cost $90 \ \text{p}$. Two drinks and a box of popcorn together cost $\pounds 1.45$. What does a box of popcorn cost?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Cost of 1 drink}  &= \pounds 1.45 - 90 \ \text{p} \\\\
                        &= 145 \ \text{p} - 90 \ \text{p} \\\\
                        &= 55 \ \text{p} \\\\
\text{Cost of popcorn}  &= 90 - 55  \\\\
                        &= 35 \ \text{p}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$35 \ \text {p}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

                        &= 145 \ \text{p} - 90 \ \text{p} \\\\
                        &= 55 \ \text{p} \\\\

                        &= 35 \ \text{p}

</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Theo bought a choclate bar for $72\ \text{p}$ and some balloons, each of which cost $20\ \text{p}$. He paid with a $\pounds10$ note and recieved $\pounds3.88$. How many balloons did he buy?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Theo bought a choclate bar for $72\ \text{p}$ and some balloons, each of which cost $20\ \text{p}$. He paid with a $\pounds10$ note and recieved $\pounds3.88$. How many balloons did he buy?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Money spent}          &= 10 - 3.88 \\\\
                            &= \pounds 6.12 \\\\
                            &= 612 \ \text{p} \\\\
\text{Cost of balloons}     &= 612 - 72 \\\\
                            &= 540 \ \text{p} \\\\
\text{Number of balloons}   &= \dfrac{540}{20} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac{20 \times 27}{20} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac{\cancel{20} \times 27}{\cancel{20}} \\\\
                            &= 27
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$27$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How many $\pounds 5$ notes are needed to make $\pounds 1000$?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How many $\pounds 5$ notes are needed to make $\pounds 1000$?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Notes needed}    &= \dfrac {1000} {5} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {200 \times 5} {5} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {200 \times \cancel {5}} {\cancel {5}} \\\\
                        &= 200
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$200$

</div>
</div>

</div>

\text {Notes needed}    &= \dfrac {1000} {5} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {200 \times 5} {5} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {200 \times \cancel {5}} {\cancel {5}} \\\\
                        &= 200
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How much change would I get from $\pounds 20$ if I bought $3$ pens at $\pounds 1.60$ each, $2$ pencils at $62\ \text{p}$ and a magazine for $\pounds4.50$?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How much change would I get from $\pounds 20$ if I bought $3$ pens at $\pounds 1.60$ each, $2$ pencils at $62\ \text{p}$ and a magazine for $\pounds4.50$?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent}     &= 3 \times \pounds 1.60 + 2 \times 62 \ \text{p} + \pounds 4.50 \\\\
                        &= 3 \times \pounds 1.60 + 2 \times \pounds 0.62 + \pounds 4.50 \\\\
                        &= 4.80 + 1.24 + 4.50 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 10.54 \\\\
\text {Change received} &= 20 - 10.54 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 9.46
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 9.46$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A maths book cost $\pounds 15.49$. How much change do you get if you pay only with $\pounds 1$ coins?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A maths book cost $\pounds 15.49$. How much change do you get if you pay only with $\pounds 1$ coins?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Amount paid}     &= 16 \times 1 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 16 \\\\
\text {Change received} &= 16 - 15.49 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 0.51 \\\\
                        &= 51 \ \text{p}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$51 \ \text{p}$

</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class="papername">
<p>latymer-upper-school--11-plus--maths--2014--sample-paper-1:1:19</p>
</div>
<div class="rag">
<p>Waffles Woof</p>
</div>
<div class="rag">
<p>rag_wf_g4</p>
</div>
<div class="rag">
<p>rag_wf_prns</p>
</div>
<div class="topics">
<ul>
<li>
tmoney
</li>
<li>
tcoin
</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How many different ways are there of paying exactly $20\ \text{p}$ using only $1\ \text{p}$ and $2\ \text{p}$ coins?

- $2$
- $11$
- $10$
- $5$
- $20$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How many different ways are there of paying exactly $20\ \text{p}$ using only $1\ \text{p}$ and $2\ \text{p}$ coins?
<emptyline>
- $2$
- $11$
- $10$
- $5$
- $20$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let's tabulate the number of ways of paying:

| $2 \ \text{p}$ coins | $1 \ \text{p}$ coins | Total |
|:--------------------:|:--------------------:|:-----:|
| $0$  | $20$ | $0 \times 2 + 20 \times 1 = 20 \ \text{p}$ |
| $1$  | $18$ | $1 \times 2 + 18 \times 1 = 20 \ \text{p}$ |
| $2$  | $16$ | $2 \times 2 + 16 \times 1 = 20 \ \text{p}$ |
| $3$  | $14$ | $3 \times 2 + 14 \times 1 = 20 \ \text{p}$ |
| $4$  | $12$ | $4 \times 2 + 12 \times 1 = 20 \ \text{p}$ |
| $5$  | $10$ | $5 \times 2 + 10 \times 1 = 20 \ \text{p}$ |
| $6$  | $8$  | $6 \times 2 + 8 \times 1 = 20 \ \text{p}$  |
| $7$  | $6$  | $7 \times 2 + 6 \times 1 = 20 \ \text{p}$  |
| $8$  | $4$  | $8 \times 2 + 4 \times 1 = 20 \ \text{p}$  |
| $9$  | $2$  | $9 \times 2 + 2 \times 1 = 20 \ \text{p}$  |
| $10$ | $0$  | $10 \times 2 + 0 \times 1 = 20 \ \text{p}$ |

Total ways $= 11$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="option" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Option**

<p>B</p>
</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$11$

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How many different ways are there of paying exactly $15\ \text{p}$ using $1\ \text{p}$ and/or $2\ \text{p}$ pieces? 

A: $2$ 

B: $3$ 

C: $7$ 

D: $8$ 

E: $15$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

</div>

B: $3$ 
<emptyline>
C: $7$ 
<emptyline>
D: $8$ 
<emptyline>
E: $15$
</code></pre>
</div>

| :---: | :---: | :---:|

| $1$ | $13$ | $1 \times 2 + 13 \times 1 = 15 \ \text{p}$ |
| $2$ | $11$ | $2 \times 2 + 11 \times 1 = 15 \ \text{p}$ |

| $6$  | $8$  | $6 \times 10 + 8 \times 5 = 100 \ \text{p}$ |

| $8$  | $4$  | $8 \times 10 + 4 \times 5 = 100 \ \text{p}$ |
| $9$  | $2$  | $9 \times 10 + 2 \times 5 = 100 \ \text{p}$ |
| $10$ | $0$  | $10 \times 10 + 0 \times 5 = 100 \ \text{p}$ |
<emptyline>
Total ways $= 11$
</code></pre>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Find the cost of $3$ adult return tickets.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Find the cost of $3$ adult return tickets.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">

</div>

<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Cost of } 3 \text{ adult tickets} &= 29.40 \times 3 \\\\
                                        &= \pounds 88.20
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The price of a childrens ticket is half that of an adult ticket. What is the cost of a return ticket for a child travelling from Leicester to Cambridge?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The price of a childrens ticket is half that of an adult ticket. What is the cost of a return ticket for a child travelling from Leicester to Cambridge?
</code></pre>

<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Cost of child ticket} &= \dfrac {1} {2} \times 29.40 \\\\
&= \pounds 14.70
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$\pounds 14.70$

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

James was given $\pounds 15$ to buy supplies for the university year. He chose to spend it on pens and pencils

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

If James buys $5$ pens and $7$ pencils, how much money will he have left?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">If James buys $5$ pens and $7$ pencils, how much money will he have left?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent}             &= 5 \times \pounds 1.25 + 7 \times 80 \ \text{p} \\\\
                                &= 5 \times \pounds 1.25 + 7 \times \pounds 0.80 \\\\
                                &= 6.25 + 5.60 \\\\
                                &= \pounds 11.85 \\\\
\text{Money left}               &= 15 - 11.85 \\\\
                                &= \pounds 3.15
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 3.15$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

If instead James buys a set of $5$ pencils, what is the maximum number of pens he can buy?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">If instead James buys a set of $5$ pencils, what is the maximum number of pens he can buy?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent}                     &= 5 \times 80 \ \text{p} \\\\
                                        &= 5 \times \pounds 0.80 \\\\
                                &= \pounds 4 \\\\
\text {Money left}                       &= 15 - 4 \\\\
                                         &= \pounds 11 \\\\
\text{Maximum pens} &= \dfrac {11} {1.25} \\\\
                                         &= 8.8 \\\\
                                         &\approx 8
\end{aligned}
$

Maximum pens $= 8$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$8$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_note_md_unit
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent}                     &= 5 \times 80 \ \text{p} \\\\
                                        &= 5 \times \pounds 0.80 \\\\
                                &= \pounds 4 \\\\
\text {Money left}                       &= 15 - 4 \\\\
                                         &= \pounds 11 \\\\
\text{Maximum pens} &= \dfrac {11} {1.25} \\\\
                                         &= 8.8 \\\\
                                         &\approx 8
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Maximum pens $= 8$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

If instead James ends up with $\pounds 2.80$ left of his money how many pens and how many pencils did he buy?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">If instead James ends up with $\pounds 2.80$ left of his money how many pens and how many pencils did he buy?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the number of pens $= a$

Let the number of pencils $= b$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
1.25 \times a + 0.80 \times b                   &= 15 - 2.80 \\\\
1.25a + 0.80b                                   &= 12.20 \\\\
100 \times 1.25a + 100 \times 0.80b             &= 100 \times 12.20 \\\\
125a + 80b                                      &= 1220 \\\\
\dfrac {125a} {5} + \dfrac {80b} {5}            &= \dfrac {1220} {5} \\\\
25a + 16b                                       &= 244 \\\\
b                                              &= \dfrac {244 - 25a} {16}
\end{aligned}
$

We can easily infer the values by using trial and error.

We try sensible values of $a$. For example, $244 - 25a$ has to be an even number. If it is an odd number, it won't satisfy the equation.

| $a$ (pens) |   $244 - 25a$   | Odd or Even? |  $b$   | $b$ whole number? |
|:----------:|:----------------:|:-------------:|:------------------------:|:------------------:|
|     $1$      | $244 - 25 \times 1 = 219$ | Odd           | —                        | No                |
|     $2$      | $244 - 25 \times 2 = 194$ | Even          | $194 \div 16 = 12.12$    | No                |
|     $3$      | $244 - 25 \times 3 = 169$ | Odd           | —                        | No                |
|     $4$      | $244 - 25 \times 4 = 144$| Even          | $144 \div 16 = 9$        | Yes              |
|     $5$      | $244 - 25 \times 5 =119$ | Odd           | —                        | No                |
|     $6$      | $244 - 25 \times 6 = 94$ | Even          | $94 \div 16 = 5.88$      | No                |
|     $7$      | $244 - 25 \times 7 = 69$ | Odd           | —                        | No                |
|     $8$      | $244 - 25 \times 8 = 44$ | Even          | $44 \div 16 = 2.75$      | No                |
|     $9$      | $244 - 25 \times 9 = 19$ | Odd           | —                        | No                |

A quick check shows that $a =4$ and $ b =9$ satisfies the equation above.

Pens $= 4$

Pencils $= 9$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Pencils $=$ $9$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Pens $=$ $4$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_note_md_unit
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Let the number of pens $= a$
<emptyline>

<emptyline>

<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="answercodeblock_title">

**Question**

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>

</code></pre>

</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 10\% \ \text{of} \ \pounds 120 \\\\
&= \dfrac {10} {100} \times 120 \\\\
&= \dfrac {10 \times 120} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1 \cancel{0} \times 12 \cancel{0}} {1 \cancel{0} \cancel {0}} \\\\
&= \pounds 12
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

</code></pre>

</div>

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 2 \dfrac {1} {2} \% \ \text{of} \ \pounds 120 \\\\

&= \dfrac {\cancel {5} \times \cancel {20} \times \cancel 2 \times 3} {\cancel 2 \times \cancel {5} \times \cancel {20}} \\\\
&= \pounds 3
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Use your answer to find $17 \dfrac {1} {2} \%$ of $\pounds 120?$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Use your answer to find $17 \dfrac {1} {2} \%$ of $\pounds 120?$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Using answers from above,

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 17 \dfrac {1} {2} \% \ \text{of} \ \pounds 120 \\\\
&= (10 \% + 5 \% + 2 \dfrac {1} {2} \%) \times 120 \\\\
&= 10 \% \times 120 + 5 \% \times  120 + 2 \dfrac {1} {2} \% \times  120 \\\\
&= 12 + 6 + 3 \\\\
&= \pounds 21
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Using answers from above,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
&= 17 \dfrac {1} {2} \% \ \text{of} \ \pounds 120 \\\\
&= (10 \% + 5 \% + 2 \dfrac {1} {2} \%) \times 120 \\\\
&= 10 \% \times 120 + 5 \% \times  120 + 2 \dfrac {1} {2} \% \times  120 \\\\
&= 12 + 6 + 3 \\\\
&= \pounds 21
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>

**Question**

Put a circle around each the amounts that can be made with four coins.

$24 \ \text{p} \quad 38 \ \text{p} \quad 57 \ \text{p} \quad \pounds 1.18 \quad \pounds 1.26$

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Alex puts his photographs in an album. $6$ photographs fit on each page.

How many pages does he use?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Alex puts his photographs in an album. $6$ photographs fit on each page.
<emptyline>
How many pages does he use?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
</div>

</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total pages}          &= \dfrac {144} {6} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {24 \times 2 \times 3} {2 \times 3} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {24 \times \cancel {2} \times \cancel {3}} {\cancel {2} \times \cancel {3}} \\\\
                            &= 24
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>

<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$24$

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Football cards are sold in packs. 

Each pack contains $8$ cards.

How many packs of football cards did Ryan buy altogether?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Football cards are sold in packs. 
<emptyline>
Each pack contains $8$ cards.
<emptyline>
How many packs of football cards did Ryan buy altogether?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Number of packs}            &= \dfrac {\text {Total cards}} {\text {Cards in a pack}} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {128} {8} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {16 \times 8} {8} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {16 \times \cancel{8}} {\cancel{8}} \\\\
                                   &= 16
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$16$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Circle the amounts below which can be made using three UK coins

$71 \ \text{p} \quad 72 \ \text{p} \quad 73 \ \text{p} \quad 74 \ \text{p} \quad 75 \ \text{p}$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Circle the amounts below which can be made using three UK coins
<emptyline>
$71 \ \text{p} \quad 72 \ \text{p} \quad 73 \ \text{p} \quad 74 \ \text{p} \quad 75 \ \text{p}$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
71 \ \text{p}    &= 50 \ \text{p} + 20 \ \text{p} + 1 \ \text{p} \\\\
72 \ \text{p}    &= 50 \ \text{p} + 20 \ \text{p} + 2 \ \text{p} \\\\
73 \ \text{p}    &= \text{Not possible} \\\\
74 \ \text{p}    &= \text{Not possible} \\\\
75 \ \text{p}    &= 50 \ \text{p} + 20 \ \text{p} + 5 \ \text{p}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$71 \ \text{p}, 72 \ \text{p}, 75 \ \text{p}$

</div>
</div>

**Question**

Work out how many units of gas were used in the $3$ months from October to January.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

</code></pre>

</div>
</div>

                    &= 780 \ \text {units}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">

$780 \ \text {units}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The cost of the gas is $7 \ \text{p}$ for each unit. Also there is a fixed charge of $\pounds 12.50$ to be paid. Work out the total cost of the gas and the fixed charge.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The cost of the gas is $7 \ \text{p}$ for each unit. Also there is a fixed charge of $\pounds 12.50$ to be paid. Work out the total cost of the gas and the fixed charge.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total cost}      &= 780 \times 7 \ \text{p} + \pounds 12.50 \\\\
                        &= 780 \times \pounds 0.07 + \pounds 12.50 \\\\
                        &= 54.60 + 12.50 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 67.10
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 67.10$

</div>
</div>

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

If $10 \%$ is added for tax, then the total cost will be $100 + 10 = 110 \%$ of the original cost. 

Using that, we get

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Final cost}  &= 110 \% \times 67.10 \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {110} {100} \times 67.10 \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {110 \times 67.10} {100} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {7381} {100} \\\\
                    &= \pounds 73.81
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">

$

\text{Tax}          &= 10 \% \text{ of } \pounds 67.10 \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {10} {100} \times 67.10 \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {10 \times 67.10} {100} \\\\
                    &= \pounds 6.71 \\\\
\text{Final cost}   &= 67.10 + 6.71 \\\\
                    &= \pounds 73.81
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The Yummy Jam Company makes jam for a wide range of shops and supermarkets. The cost of a jar of jam is made up by adding the costs of the jam, the jar and the lid. 

So 

Total cost of a jar of jam = Cost of the jar + Cost of the lid + Cost of the jam

At present, the company calculates that the cost of the jam is $20 \ \text{p}$ for every $100 \ \text{g}$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

<emptyline>

</code></pre>
</div>

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

For a small jar, containing $200 \ \text{g}$ of jam, the lid costs $10 \ \text{p}$ and the jar $50 \ \text{p}$. What is the total cost of the jar of jam?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">For a small jar, containing $200 \ \text{g}$ of jam, the lid costs $10 \ \text{p}$ and the jar $50 \ \text{p}$. What is the total cost of the jar of jam?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Cost of 100 \text{g} jam}     &= 20 \\\\
\text{Cost of 1 \text{g} jam}       &= \dfrac{20}{100} \\\\
                                    &= 0.20 \ \text{p} \\\\
\text{Cost of 200 \text{g} jam}     &= 200 \times \dfrac{20}{100} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac{200 \times 20}{100} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac{2 \cancel{00} \times 20}{1\cancel{00}} \\\\
                                    &= 2 \times 20 \\\\
                                    &= 40 \ \text{p} \\\\
\text{Total cost}                   &= 50 + 10  + 40  \\\\
                                    &= 100 \ \text{p} \\\\
                                    &= \pounds 1.00
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 1.00$

</div>
</div>

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

                                    &= 0.20 \ \text{p} \\\\

                                    &= \dfrac{200 \times 20}{100} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac{2 \cancel{00} \times 20}{1\cancel{00}} \\\\
                                    &= 2 \times 20 \\\\
                                    &= 40 \ \text{p} \\\\
\text{Total cost}                   &= 50 + 10  + 40  \\\\

</div>

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A medium jar contains $350 \ \text{g}$ of jam and the medium jar costs $60 \ \text{p}$. If the total cost is $\pounds 1.55$, what is the cost of the lid for the medium jar of jam?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A medium jar contains $350 \ \text{g}$ of jam and the medium jar costs $60 \ \text{p}$. If the total cost is $\pounds 1.55$, what is the cost of the lid for the medium jar of jam?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Using the cost of jam per gram calculated in the previous question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Cost of the lid} &= \pounds 1.55 - 350 \times 0.20 \ \text{p} - 60 \ \text{p} \\\\
                        &= 155 \ \text{p} - 70 \ \text{p} - 60 \ \text{p} \\\\
                        &= 155 - 70 - 60 \\\\
                        &= 155 - 130 \\\\
                        &= 25 \ \text{p}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$25 \ \text{p}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Using the cost of jam per gram calculated in the previous question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Cost of the lid} &= \pounds 1.55 - 350 \times 0.20 \ \text{p} - 60 \ \text{p} \\\\
                        &= 155 \ \text{p} - 70 \ \text{p} - 60 \ \text{p} \\\\
                        &= 155 - 70 - 60 \\\\
                        &= 155 - 130 \\\\
                        &= 25 \ \text{p}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">

</div>

<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$25 \ \text{p}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A large jar of jam contains $500 \ \text{g}$ of jam and the total cost is $\pounds 3.00$. If the jar costs four times as much as the lid, what does the jar cost?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A large jar of jam contains $500 \ \text{g}$ of jam and the total cost is $\pounds 3.00$. If the jar costs four times as much as the lid, what does the jar cost?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the cost of lid $= a$

Cost of the jar $= 4a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
4a + a + 500 \times 0.20 \ \text{p}     &= \pounds 3 \\\\
5a + 100 \ \text{p}                    &= 300 \ \text{p} \\\\
5a                                      &= 300 - 100 \\\\
5a                                      &= 200 \\\\
a                                       &= \dfrac {200} {5} \\\\
a                                       &= \dfrac {5 \times 40} {5} \\\\
a                                       &= \dfrac {\cancel {5} \times 40} {\cancel {5}} \\\\
a                                       &= 40 \ \text{p} \\\\
\text {Cost of jar}                     &= 4a \\\\
                                        &= 4 \times 40 \\\\
                                        &= 160 \ \text{p} \\\\
                                        &= \pounds 1.60
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 1.60$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

$

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A catering jar with a lid costing $65  \ \text{p}$ and a jar costing $\pounds 2$ has a total cost of $\pounds 4.65$. What weight of jam does the catering jar contain?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A catering jar with a lid costing $65  \ \text{p}$ and a jar costing $\pounds 2$ has a total cost of $\pounds 4.65$. What weight of jam does the catering jar contain?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the weight of jam $= w$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\pounds 2 + 65 \ \text{p} + 0.20 \times w           &= \pounds 4.65 \\\\
200 \ \text{p} + 65 \ \text{p} + 0.20 \times w      &= 465 \ \text{p} \\\\
0.2w + 265                                          &= 465 \\\\
0.2w                                                &= 465 - 265 \\\\
0.2w                                                &= 200 \\\\
w                                                   &= \dfrac {200} {0.2} \\\\
w                                                   &= \dfrac {200 \times 10} {0.2 \times 10} \\\\
w                                                   &= \dfrac {2000} {2} \\\\
w                                                   &= \dfrac {2 \times 1000} {2} \\\\
w                                                   &= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times 1000} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
w                                                   &= 1000 \ \text{g} \\\\
w                                                   &= 1 \ \text{kg}
\end{aligned}
$

Weight of jam $= 1 \ \text{kg}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$1 \ \text{kg}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Let the weight of jam $= w$
<emptyline>

w                                                   &= 1000 \ \text{g} \\\\

\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Weight of jam $= 1 \ \text{kg}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

![papers/the-queens-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q09-i00.png](/assets/papers/the-queens-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q09-i00.png "papers/the-queens-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q09-i00.png")

I have FIVE coins in my pocket. 

They add up to $77 \ \text{p}$.

What are the coins?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

| $50 \ \text{p} + 20 \ \text{p} + 5 \ \text{p} + 1 \ \text{p} + 1 \ \text{p} = 77 \ \text{p}$   |
| $50 \ \text{p} + 10 \ \text{p} + 10 \ \text{p} + 5 \ \text{p} + 2 \ \text{p} = 77 \ \text{p}$  |
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">

$50 \ \text{p}, \ 10 \ \text{p}, \ 10 \ \text{p}, \ 5 \ \text{p}, \ 2 \ \text{p}$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
